\chapter{Business Capabilities}\label{model:bc}
The hierarchy below contains only explicit governed BC tier and parent assertions. Full definitions, decomposition assertions, boundaries, illustrative activities, care contexts, exclusions and stopping points are reproduced in \hyperref[rad:business]{the BC source detail}. Relevance is not inherited. Regions are contextual groupings.

Product procurement and supply context is retained without inferring further Health Product Management decomposition.
